# Paper numbers

Generated by `scripts/analysis/paper_numbers.py` from the summary files only; do not edit by hand. Each row: estimate, 95% CI (cluster bootstrap over trajectories for Study 1; paired t and sequence bootstrap for Study 2; Wilson for D42), raw p (cluster sign-flip, or paired t), Holm-adjusted p where a Holm family exists, n (items / trajectories, sequences, or calls), item set, and the exact source key (`<file>:<path>`). Rows without an interval are rates, counts, flags or spend. Interpretation is not part of this file.

Sources:

- `V` = `results/summaries/verification.json` (SHA-256 `da1307ef9d7e25e423edd2bb8cda287f574c05aee278214e89f8985d127ce527`)
- `S2` = `results/summaries/study2_analysis.json` (SHA-256 `e63549af60b37eb783db29b2c2a15d4ffdf60989a8cb654bfb821037ccb959bd`)
- `S2b` = `results/summaries/study2_a05b_sensitivities.json` (SHA-256 `40755d213ee28300e06914918912349d7f9a8c11d079fb6977d9a0b12b29a654`)
- `D40` = `results/summaries/sensitivity_residue.json` (SHA-256 `77743bb91a67785effd7e557dc0bf0ea7058bf1805e33dedcecfc4bede97a77b`)
- `D42` = `results/summaries/d42_accept_classes.json` (SHA-256 `656fd54a7c9f48046b1c487bc8450b2e2db7e585fbe11e09c4cd934a20f4d3a1`)
- `P10` = `results/summaries/p10_projection.json` (SHA-256 `5601f6070d3620624bd85cbdaad6b405712714ed24e83ed8b5dbdf4cdffa7088`)
